RE 110833Z — Two 2k searches on color-reflected copies of the same board did worse than a single 4k search. On 384 natural roots, against the mean of the two independent Stockfish500k orientation targets, mean absolute score-probability error was .119477 for the two-search label and .113944 for original-orientation 4k. Relative error INCREASED 4.8558% [1.4855,8.4873]. Using reflected 4k as the control gives a 2.1734% increase [−0.8319,5.4218]. Both one-orientation 500k references also disfavor the pair over original 4k. At 2M on the 48 frozen roots, the increase versus original 4k was 1.1075% [−4.4156,7.1470]; versus reflected 4k 6.3203% [0.3140,13.5570]. No two-search training slot.

The symmetry was real: native ordered feature lists, integer sums and raw r67 static scores were identical on all 384 boards. All 10,663 legal child moves corresponded under the transformation. The five chess-rule controls passed, including both castlings, en passant, all four promotions and checkmate. A fullmove counter numbers Black moves; therefore reflected child boards match all five game-relevant fields, while the original and doubly reflected complete FENs match all six. Shallow search returns a different numeric score on 355/384 orientation pairs and a different mapped move on 148/384. Averaging helped versus original 2k alone (+2.16% relative), but the single reflected 2k search was already slightly better on average. This does not justify spending the extra search.

In the original interleaved experiment the two calls used 1.030× the child search CPU of the original 4k and 1.252× including both TT resets. Two further full schedules with reversed order reproduced all 3,072 engine outputs across both schedules. The corresponding reset-inclusive CPU ratios were 1.188 and 1.189 (search CPU .982 and .999). All miss the prespecified ≤1.10 reset-inclusive bar. The two 4k and two 2k orientations per root, two independent 500k refs each plus two 2M refs on 48, total 2,400 primary cells. Eight fresh-root sentinels repeated each cell requested in their original row. Reproduce via 110833Z; the raw score/bestmove/depth/nodes/WDL and timing ledgers are below. No change to current A19 or to the production W5 generator.
